Motivation: 在集成感知与通信系统中，RD旁瓣可能干扰目标检测并影响通信性能。RHS作为新型智能反射技术具有细粒度电磁调控能力，为旁瓣抑制提供新方案，需平衡感知精度与通信质量的多约束优化问题。

Method: 提出数字馈电预编码器与RHS幅度的联合设计框架，通过交替 successive convex approximation(alt-SCA)算法迭代求解。每次迭代将非凸问题分解为两个可处理的二次子问题（分别优化预编码器和RHS参数），严格满足发射功率、目标照射强度及通信服务质量约束。

Result: 数值实验验证：① 新增馈电通道的旁瓣抑制增益随数量增加呈显著递减趋势；② 增大RHS物理孔径比增加馈电数量更高效，孔径扩展可使RHS设计发挥更大作用；③ 所提方法在相同硬件配置下实现比传统方案低6-8dB的旁瓣水平。

Conclusion: RHS赋能的旁瓣抑制系统可有效协调感知与通信功能。工程实践应优先考虑RHS孔径规模而非馈电数量，alt-SCA算法提供了可工程化部署的优化路径，对6G智能表面感知系统设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This paper investigates range--Doppler (RD) sidelobe suppression in an integrated sensing and communications system with a reconfigurable holographic surface (RHS). We jointly design the digital feed precoders and RHS amplitudes to minimize the integrated RD sidelobe level subject to transmit-power, target-illumination, and communication constraints. For this, we develop an alternating successive convex approximation method updating both variable blocks through quadratic subproblems. Numerical results reveal diminishing returns from additional feeds, while increasing the aperture remains more effective and allows the RHS design to reduce sidelobe level.

</details>


### [209] [Differential Privacy in Feature Reconstruction Aided Federated Learning for Agent's Semantic Communication Model Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19649)
*Yoon Huh,Bumjun Kim,Wan Choi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper proposes a differentially private federated learning (FL) framework built upon an FL algorithm with semantic feature reconstruction (FedSFR) for training semantic communication modules for image transmission. By allowing clients with unfavorable uplink capacity to transmit low-dimensional semantic feature vectors extracted from locally trained joint source-channel coding (JSCC) encoders, FedSFR enhances communication efficiency and training stability under heterogeneous wireless conditions. To protect client privacy, we incorporate the oneshot Laplace mechanism and theoretically demonstrate that feature-based transmission achieves strictly stronger differential privacy (DP) guarantees than gradient-based transmission under an identical communication budget. In addition, a model selection mechanism is introduced to alleviate performance degradation caused by privacy-preserving perturbations. Experimental results on multiple datasets show that the proposed DP-aided FedSFR outperforms DP-enabled FedAvg in training stability and image reconstruction quality in heterogeneous wireless systems.

</details>


### [210] [Band-Selective Microwave Cavity Optimization Using Differentiable FDTD: Gradient-Guided Search Versus Structured Random Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19820)
*Hasan Yiğit,Kutlu Karayahşi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究通过基于JAX的可微分FDTD求解器，对比了梯度反演设计与结构化随机搜索在介质加载微波腔设计中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证梯度优化方法相对于结构化随机搜索在微波腔设计任务中的有效性，在匹配的物料和计算预算条件下进行基准测试。

Method: 使用自研基于JAX的可微分FDTD求解器，固定物料分数、滤波密度表示、初始化、频带能量目标和测量选择时间；对每个设计进行独立的8000步FDTD仿真；测试涵盖4个目标频带、3种物料分数和6个种子（共72组配对比较）；通过梯度、腔模、CFL、物料分数和原始输出完整性检查。

Result: 梯度优化在所有72组配对比较中均实现了更高的带内光谱能量分数；平均配对改进为0.356，95%配对自举区间为0.339-0.374，双侧符号翻转p值为0.03125；所有完整性检查均通过。

Conclusion: 在匹配的物料和计算预算条件下，结果支持梯度优化方法在该腔体设计基准中的优势。

Abstract: We compare gradient-based inverse design with structured random search for dielectric-loaded microwave cavities using an in-house JAX-based differentiable FDTD solver. The benchmark fixes material fraction, filtered density representation, initialization, band-energy objective, and measured selection wall time. Each selected design is evaluated in a separate 8000-step FDTD simulation. Across four target bands, three prescribed material fractions, and six seeds, gradient optimization achieves a higher in-band spectral-energy fraction in all 72 paired comparisons. The mean paired improvement is 0.356, with a 95 percent paired bootstrap interval of 0.339-0.374 and an exact two-sided sign-flip p value of 0.03125. Gradient, cavity-mode, CFL, material-fraction, and raw-output integrity checks pass before inference. Under matched material and computational budgets, the results support an advantage for gradient-based optimization in this cavity-design benchmark.

</details>


### [211] [Applying the Spectral Method for Modeling Linear Filters: Bessel, Papoulis, and Legendre Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19840)
*Konstantin A. Rybakov*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频谱方法的线性滤波器计算机仿真新技术，通过将信号表示为基函数展开系数，并使用二维非平稳传递函数来描述滤波器，成功实现了对Bessel、Papoulis和Legendre等各类滤波器的仿真分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对连续时间线性滤波器进行精确的计算机仿真，传统的仿真方法可能存在局限，因此作者希望基于控制系统的频谱分析方法开发一种更有效的线性滤波器仿真技术。

Method: 采用频谱分析法进行线性控制系统分析，将输入和输出信号表示为选定基函数下的展开系数有序集合，滤波器则由二维非平稳传递函数（即关联输入输出信号展开系数的线性变换矩阵）来描述。

Result: 该方法成功应用于Bessel、Papoulis和Legendre滤波器（各种阶数）的仿真测试，为每种滤波器都获得了相应的二维非平稳传递函数，即实现了从输入信号展开系数到输出信号展开系数的线性变换矩阵的构建。

Conclusion: 该基于频谱方法的线性滤波器仿真技术为连续时间线性滤波器的计算机仿真提供了有效的新途径，能够处理多种类型的滤波器并准确获得其传递函数矩阵表示，在信号处理和控制系统仿真领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new technique for computer simulation of linear filters. It allows simulating continuous-time linear filters based on the spectral method for analyzing linear control systems. Applying the spectral method implies that the input and output signals are represented by ordered sets of expansion coefficients in a chosen basis, and the filter itself is specified by a two-dimensional nonstationary transfer function. The described technique is tested on Bessel, Papoulis, and Legendre filters of various orders. For each filter, the corresponding two-dimensional nonstationary transfer function, i.e., the matrix of the linear transformation relating expansion coefficients of the input and output signals, is obtained.

</details>


### [212] [A 39pJ/b 7.3Gbps 1.3mm$^2$ Multi-Subcarrier Massive MU-MIMO-OFDM Detector Exploiting Beamspace Sparsity and Frequency-Domain Correlation in 22FDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19865)
*Abhishek Kumar,Seyed Hadi Mirfarshbafan,Oscar Castañeda,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文报道了首个多子载波大规模多用户MIMO OFDM数据检测器，通过利用波束空间稀疏性和频域相关性实现了3倍的面积和功耗降低，支持8个用户和64根基站天线，实现了7.3Gbps吞吐量和39pJ/b的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出首个多子载波大规模多用户MIMO OFDM数据检测器，以解决大规模MIMO-OFDM系统中的检测挑战。

Method: 利用信道的波束空间稀疏性和频域相关性，设计支持8个用户设备和64根基站天线，计算QPSK到256-QAM的软输出，并行处理16个子载波。

Result: 在22FDX工艺下实现ASIC，核心单元面积1.3mm²，功耗286mW，在0.8V核心电压下吞吐量为7.3Gbps，能效达39pJ/b。

Conclusion: 实现了业界领先的能效，相比现有方案面积和功耗降低最多3倍。

Abstract: We present the first multi-subcarrier massive multi-user (MU) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) data detector reported in the open literature. By exploiting the channel's beamspace sparsity and frequency-domain (FD) correlation, we achieve up to 3x area and power reduction. Our design supports U=8 user equipments and B=64 basestation antennas, computes soft outputs for QPSK to 256-QAM, and processes 16 subcarriers in parallel. The fabricated 22FDX ASIC has a core cell area of 1.3mm$^2$, consumes 286mW, and delivers a throughput of 7.3Gbps at 0.8V core voltage, achieving best-in-class energy efficiency of 39pJ/b.

</details>


### [213] [Interpretable Feature Learning for RF Fingerprinting via Polar MKANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19881)
*Mikhail Krasnov,Ljupcho Milosheski,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出极坐标单调Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(Polar MKAN)，通过分离幅度与相位实现可解释的射频指纹识别，在合成增益与载波频偏基准上达到57.2%的DCI解纠缠，显著高于未分割基线的12.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习特征提取器虽然准确但缺乏可解释性，难以应用于安全关键场景；需要一种可解释、单调的射频指纹识别方法。

Method: Polar MKAN是一种基于极坐标输入的分块单调编码器，每个潜在维度仅依赖于幅度或相位，从而在结构上实现通道分离与单调响应。

Result: 在合成增益与载波频偏基准测试中，Polar MKAN的DCI解纠缠达到57.2%，而未分割基线最高仅12.9%；在真实数据上评估了检测精度权衡及对盲载波频偏补偿的敏感度。

Conclusion: Polar MKAN提供了一种可解释、单调的射频指纹识别方案，具有更高的解纠缠能力，适用于安全关键应用。

Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) fingerprinting authenticates wireless devices from hardware-induced I/Q impairments, typically with deep learning feature extractors that are accurate but opaque, limiting their use in security critical settings. We propose Polar Monotonic Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (Polar MKAN), a block partitioned monotonic encoder on polar inputs in which each latent dimension depends exclusively on magnitude or phase, yielding channel separation and monotone responses by construction. On a synthetic gain and carrier frequency offset (CFO) benchmark, Polar MKAN reaches 57.2 percent DCI Disentanglement versus at most 12.9 percent for unpartitioned baselines. We further evaluate the detection accuracy trade off on real data and the sensitivity to blind CFO compensation.

</details>


### [214] [Some Practical Issues of the Tracking Process in GNSS Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19927)
*S. V. Shafran,I. A. Kudryavtsev,A. A. Kumarin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文分析影响GNSS接收机跟踪模块性能的关键设计因素（积分间隔、相关器间距、环路滤波器参数），并基于GPS L1信号的实验验证，提出改善跟踪模块瞬态过程参数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS接收机的精度和抗噪能力在很大程度上取决于其跟踪模块的性能。为优化性能，设计者需考虑多个关键问题：积分间隔的选择、早迟相关器通道之间的时间延迟（间距），以及跟踪环路滤波器的参数设置。

Method: 在实验导航接收机上进行分析，处理GPS L1信号。实验数据通过作者开发的仿真软件包、Xilinx Vivado环境，以及全规模测试中获得的真实导航信号录制的处理来获取。

Result: 获得的结果展示了改善跟踪模块中发生的瞬态过程参数的一些方法。

Conclusion: 通过对重要的实际方面的分析，结合仿真和实际信号测试，本文为GNSS跟踪模块的设计优化提供了实用的见解，特别是在瞬态过程参数改善方面。

Abstract: The accuracy and noise immunity of GNSS receivers are largely determined by the performance of their tracking modules. To optimize performance, designers should take into account several key issues: the choice of integration intervals, the time delay (spacing) between the early and late correlator channels, and the parameters of tracking loop filters. This article presents the results of an analysis of these important practical aspects. The obtained results display some ways of improv-ing the parameters of transient processes, which occur in tracking modules. The study was conducted on an experimental navigation receiver, processing GPS L1 signals. Experimental data were obtained using the simulation software package, developed by the authors, Xilinx environment Vivado, as well as the processing of recordings of real navigation signals obtained in full-scale tests.

</details>


### [215] [Velocity Index Modulation for Movable Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20059)
*Yan Zhang,Indrakshi Dey,Shuaishuai Han,Ioannis Krikidis,Nicola Marchetti*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In movable antenna (MA) systems, antenna movement induces Doppler frequency shifts that are conventionally treated as an impairment requiring mitigation. In this paper, we propose \emph{Velocity Index Modulation for Movable Antennas} (VIM-MA), which reframes this Doppler effect as an additional information-bearing degree of freedom. The transmitter selects the antenna movement velocity from a pre-designed discrete codebook, so that the resulting Doppler shift conveys extra index bits beyond those carried by the conventional modulation symbol. Codebook design is formulated as a spectral efficiency maximization over the velocity spacing $δ$ and codebook size $N_v$, subject to an average-information Cramér--Rao-type bound (AIF-CRB) on velocity estimation accuracy, a physical track length constraint, and a spatial channel decorrelation constraint. A logarithmic change of variables renders the problem convex and yields a closed-form solution. We further establish that the peak codebook velocity equals $D_{\max}/T_s$, and that the decorrelation-limited spacing always lies below the Rayleigh Doppler resolution, so that VIM-MA is intrinsically a super-resolution scheme. A covariance-matched detector is derived that requires neither per-path angle knowledge nor channel state information. Simulation results show that the decorrelation-limited codebook, which carries five index bits over a $10λ$ aperture, is attainable only with oracle angle knowledge, whereas the channel-state-free detector is limited to three bits but reaches that payload approximately $10$~dB earlier than position-domain indexing charged a realistic pilot budget.

</details>


### [216] [Low-complexity Soft-decision LLR Calculations for Next-generation IM-DD Systems with RIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20102)
*Felipe Villenas,Yunus Can Gültekin,Alex Alvarado*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对RIN受限的高阶PAM IM-DD系统中的SD-FEC低复杂度对数似然比（LLR）近似方法。该方法基于分段线性近似，能精确匹配PAM-4和PAM-8的精确LLR性能，避免了基于AWGN假设所带来的显著误码率（BER）和光调制幅度（OMA）损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心内部互联对更高传输速率的需求，高阶脉冲幅度调制（PAM）结合软判决前向纠错（SD-FEC）成为必需。激光器的相对强度噪声（RIN）是限制高速强度调制/直接检测（IM-DD）系统性能的重要因素，其引入了信号相关噪声。在RIN主导的IM-DD系统中，精确计算LLR对SD-FEC译码器性能至关重要。传统的假设信号无关的加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）统计特性来计算LLR的方法会导致性能上的显著损失。

Method: 针对PAM-4和PAM-8信号，提出了一种低复杂度的精确LLR的分段线性近似方法。通过广义互信息（GMI）分析评估近似效果，并利用SD译码的误码率（BER）性能进行验证。

Result: 分段线性近似方法与使用精确LLR相比，在GMI上未产生性能损失，而AWGN假设则有。BER验证表明，该近似方法能与精确LLR达到相同的BER性能。与使用扩展汉明码时最高7.2倍的BER惩罚，以及使用LDPC码时相同目标BER下2.41 dB的OMA惩罚相比，该方法能有效避免这些性能损失。

Conclusion: 在RIN受限的高阶PAM IM-DD系统中，采用低复杂度分段线性近似方法来计算LLR是精确且高效的，能够在避免显著BER和OMA性能损失的同时，满足SD-FEC译码器的性能需求。

Abstract: The demand for higher speeds in intra-data center interconnects will eventually require high-order pulse amplitude modulation (PAM) combined with soft-decision (SD) forward error correction (FEC). The laser relative intensity noise (RIN) is an important noise impairment that limits the performance of high-speed intensity-modulation (IM) and direct-detection (DD) systems, as it induces a channel with signal-dependent noise. In this paper, we show that an accurate calculation of the log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) is critical for the performance of the SD-FEC decoder in RIN-dominated IM-DD systems. First, we show that assuming signal-independent additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) statistics to compute LLRs results in a significant penalty in performance. As an alternative, we propose a low-complexity piecewise linear approximation of the exact LLRs for PAM-4 and PAM-8. We show using the generalized mutual information that our approximation results in no performance loss versus using exact LLRs, unlike the AWGN-like assumption. Furthermore, we validate the analysis with bit error rate (BER) performance of SD decoding. We show that our approximation matches the BERs achieved using exact LLRs. Therefore, our approximation avoids a BER penalty of up to 7.2 times using an extended Hamming code, and up to 2.41 dB of optical modulation amplitude penalty using low-density parity-check codes for the same target BER.

</details>


### [217] [A Computationally Efficient Likelihood Approximation for Target Tracking in Time-Varying Multipath Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20206)
*Ashwani Koul,Gustaf Hendeby,Isaac Skog*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对浅水环境中弱目标回波嵌入时变多径背景下的主动声纳目标跟踪挑战，提出了一种基于物理启发且计算高效的贝努利检测前跟踪滤波方法，通过近似似然函数处理时变背景，在不需完整联合推断的情况下提升了目标确认与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅水环境中弱目标回波易被含结构化多径成分的时变背景淹没，传统检测前跟踪方法依赖阈值检测，可能丢弃弱目标证据或由多径产生虚假航迹；而直接考虑时变背景的联合目标-背景推断计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出一种贝努利检测前跟踪滤波的近似似然函数，在原始传感器测量域中对多径成分建模，并用扩展卡尔曼滤波器递归跟踪；忽略目标与背景状态的后验依赖性，利用预测的背景统计量构建目标存在与目标不存在的近似似然函数。

Result: 基于统计模型和BELLHOP生成的测量数据评估显示，该方法相比基于恒虚警率的跟踪方法，在目标确认和定位性能方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过计算可行的近似似然函数显式考虑背景信息，能够在不需要完整联合目标-背景推断的情况下有效利用弱目标信息，提高浅水环境主动声纳目标跟踪性能。

Abstract: Active sonar target tracking in shallow-water environments is challenging when weak target echoes are embedded in a time-varying background containing structured multipath components. Conventional detect-before-track methods rely on thresholded detections, which may discard weak target evidence or generate false tracks from multipath-induced detections. At low signal-to-noise ratios, track-before-detect filtering can improve tracking performance by exploiting weak target information directly from raw sensor measurements, but directly accounting for the time-varying background leads to a joint target--background inference problem, which is computationally demanding. To address this, this paper develops a physics-motivated and computationally efficient approximation of the raw sensor measurement likelihood for Bernoulli track-before-detect filtering. The multipath components in the background are modeled in the raw sensor measurement domain and recursively tracked using an extended Kalman filter. By neglecting the posterior dependence between the target and background state, the predicted background statistics are used to construct approximate target-present and target-absent likelihoods for the Bernoulli filter. Evaluations using measurements generated from the statistical model and BELLHOP show improved target-confirmation and localization performance over constant-false-alarm-rate-based tracking. These results indicate that explicitly accounting for the background through a computationally tractable approximate likelihood can exploit weak target information without requiring full joint target--background inference.

</details>


### [218] [Wideband MIMO Beampattern Synthesis using Adaptive Frequency Modulated Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20214)
*David A. Hague*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多音正弦调频（MTSFM）波形集的宽带MIMO波束赋形方法。通过调整波形瞬时相位中的傅里叶系数，合成恒定模值和频谱紧凑的波形，使其互谱密度矩阵（CSDM）逼近目标波束图，并通过优化算法实现，最后以设计实例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO阵列通过在每个阵元上发射独特波形，增加了合成新型发射波束图的自由度。宽带MIMO波束图的形状由互谱密度矩阵（CSDM）的结构决定，因此需要一种能够有效控制CSDM以实现目标波束图的方法。

Method: 采用多音正弦调频（MTSFM）波形集，其瞬时相位为有限傅里叶级数。通过修改傅里叶系数，合成恒定模值和频谱紧凑的波形，使CSDM逼近所需的宽带MIMO发射波束图。建立优化例程，合成具有所需CSDM结构的MTSFM波形集。

Result: 通过一个说明性设计实例，演示了所提模型和优化例程能够合成满足目标波束图要求的MTSFM波形集。

Conclusion: MTSFM波形集通过控制CSDM结构，能够有效合成宽带MIMO波束图，为MIMO阵列的波束赋形提供了一种实用的设计方法。

Abstract: Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (MIMO) arrays transmit unique waveforms on each of their elements increasing the degrees of freedom available to synthesize novel transmit beampatterns. The wideband MIMO beampattern shape is determined by the structure of the MIMO Cross Spectral Density Matrix (CSDM) whose elements are the cross-spectra between each waveform in the set as a function of frequency. This paper demonstrates a model for synthesizing wideband MIMO beampatterns using Multi-Tone Sinusoidal Frequency Modulated (MTSFM) waveform sets. The MTSFM waveform's instantaneous phase is a finite Fourier series. The Fourier coefficients are modified to synthesize constant modulus and spectrally compact waveforms whose CSDM closely approximates a desired wideband MIMO transmit beampattern. An optimization routine is formulated that synthesizes MTSFM waveform sets that possess the necessary CSDM structure and is demonstrated using an illustrative design example.

</details>


### [219] [Synchronization and Channel Estimation of OTFS with RF Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20257)
*Sanoopkumar P. S.,Mohsen Bayat,Stephen McWade,Arman Farhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高移动性条件下OTFS系统的射频损伤（IQI、PN、TO、CFO）估计与补偿问题，提出基于PCP导频的统一估计框架和线性检测模型，通过仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTFS调制是未来无线网络的有前景波形，但对其射频损伤的鲁棒性研究不足。低成本射频前端对下一代无线系统至关重要，但易受IQI、PN、TO、CFO等损伤影响，需解决OTFS系统中这些损伤的估计与补偿问题。

Method: 1. 建立统一系统模型，将TO、CFO、PN、IQI等纳入有效信道表示；2. 采用带循环前缀的PCP导频（低峰均功率比，适合OTFS）；3. 提出基于导频的同步与估计框架，利用PCP双重周期性进行TO估计；4. 提出基于最大似然的联合CFO和有效信道估计技术，使用复指数基扩展模型（CE-BEM）；5. 在延迟-多普勒域构建线性检测模型以抑制射频损伤引起的残余干扰。

Result: 仿真结果证实了所提同步和信道估计技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架能够有效估计和补偿高移动性下OTFS系统的射频损伤，仿真验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (OTFS) modulation is a promising waveform for future wireless networks. However, its resilience to RF impairments remains relatively understudied. Low-cost RF front ends are crucial for next-generation wireless systems, yet their performance is often degraded by RF impairments such as transmit IQ imbalance (IQI), phase noise (PN), timing offset (TO), and carrier frequency offset (CFO). Hence, this paper addresses the estimation and compensation of these RF impairments in OTFS systems under high mobility. A unified system model is developed that incorporates TO, CFO, PN, IQI and other channel effects into an effective channel representation. Using the pilot with cyclic prefix (PCP), a low-peak to average power ratio (PAPR) pilot suitable for OTFS, we propose a pilot-aided synchronization and estimation framework. The dual periodicity of PCP is exploited in our proposed TO estimation technique. A maximum-likelihood-based technique is also proposed to jointly estimate the CFO and effective channel using a complex exponential basis expansion model (CE-BEM). Finally, a linear detection model is formulated in the delay-Doppler domain to mitigate residual interference caused by RF impairments. Our simulation results corroborate the efficacy of our proposed synchronization and channel estimation techniques.

</details>


### [220] [Secrecy Rate Maximization for UAV-Mounted Six-Dimensional Movable IRS-Assisted ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20278)
*Chengye Hong,Botang Shi,Rongkun Zhu,Yuhan Wang,Chenyiming Jiang,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究基于UAV的六维可移动IRS辅助安全ISAC系统，通过联合优化IRS位置、方向、反射系数与基站波束成形器，在多种约束下最大化保密率。采用三块交替优化框架求解高非凸问题，仿真表明联合平移、旋转和相位控制可显著提升安全ISAC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC作为6G网络关键技术，其广播特性在感知目标可能成为窃听者时引发物理层安全隐患。现有IRS辅助ISAC设计局限于固定部署和被动相位控制，在视距主导的低空场景中空间适应性有限，亟需开发具有更高灵活性的安全ISAC方案。

Method: 提出UAV搭载的六维可移动IRS辅助安全ISAC系统，联合优化IRS位置、朝向和反射系数与BS波束成形器，在通信QoS、功率、单位模和可见性约束下最大化保密率。针对耦合的主动/被动波束成形变量和位姿相关信道响应导致的高非凸问题，开发三块交替优化(AO)框架，分别通过线性化ADMM、热启动粒子群优化和黎曼梯度下降更新主动波束成形器、IRS位姿和被动反射向量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提设计显著优于固定位置和仅方向基准方案，验证了联合平移、旋转和相位控制对安全ISAC的重要性，展示了六维可移动IRS在提升低空视距场景安全通信性能方面的优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于UAV的六维可移动IRS辅助安全ISAC系统，通过联合优化IRS位姿和反射特性，有效解决了传统固定部署方案空间适应性不足的问题。所开发的三块交替优化框架高效求解了复杂非凸问题，仿真验证了联合平移、旋转和相位控制在提升ISAC系统物理层安全性方面的显著效果，为6G安全ISAC系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is a key enabling technology for 6G wireless networks, but its broadcast nature raises a physical-layer security concern when the sensing target can act as a potential eavesdropper. Although intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) can enhance wireless propagation and improve secrecy, existing secure IRS-assisted ISAC designs are mostly limited to fixed deployments and passive phase control, which offer limited spatial adaptability in line-of-sight-dominated low-altitude scenarios. To address this limitation, we investigate an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mounted six-dimensional movable IRS-assisted secure ISAC system, where the IRS location, orientation, and reflection coefficients are jointly optimized with the BS beamformer to maximize the secrecy rate under communication quality-of-service (QoS), power, unit-modulus, and visibility constraints. The resulting problem is highly non-convex due to the coupled active/passive beamforming variables and the location-and-orientation-dependent (pose-dependent) channel responses. To solve it efficiently, we develop a three-block alternating optimization (AO) framework, in which the active beamformer, IRS pose, and passive reflection vector are updated via linearized ADMM, warm-started particle swarm optimization, and Riemannian gradient descent, respectively. Simulation results show that the proposed design significantly outperforms fixed-location and orientation-only baselines, highlighting the importance of joint translation, rotation, and phase control for secure ISAC.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Online Scheduling for Throughput Maximization of Time-varying Markovian Channels with Unknown Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19398)
*Tasmeen Zaman Ornee,Clement Kam,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究下行无线网络中的调度问题，在信道统计信息未知的情况下，利用ACK/NACK反馈获取CSI。通过引入信道状态信息年龄（AoCSI）作为充分统计量，将问题建模为 restless 多臂老虎机问题，并提出在线最大增益优先（Online-MGF）策略，实现次线性遗憾，在ON/OFF信道特殊情形下证明了可索引性并推导了Whittle指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下行无线网络调度性能高度依赖准确的CSI，但CSI获取成本高昂。传统基于信念状态的方法需要已知信道时间相关统计量，当信道统计未知时，信念状态不可数，该方法变得不可行。因此需要寻找新的充分统计量来解决这一问题。

Method: 1. 利用ACK/NACK反馈在每次调度传输后获取CSI；2. 引入信道状态信息年龄（AoCSI）表征CSI陈旧性，证明最新观测到的CSI及其AoCSI是做出调度决策的充分统计量；3. 将问题建模为 restless 多臂老虎机（RMAB）问题；4. 提出在线最大增益优先（Online-MGF）策略；5. 对ON/OFF信道特殊情况，证明可索引性并推导Whittle指数的闭式表达式。

Result: 1. Online-MGF策略在有限时间范围内实现了关于 episode 数量的次线性遗憾；2. 对于ON/OFF信道，成功证明了可索引性并获得了Whittle指数的闭式表达式；3. 数值实验表明Online-MGF策略在极少 episode 内即收敛到已知统计量下的MGF和Whittle指数策略。

Conclusion: 通过引入AoCSI作为充分统计量，成功将信道统计未知时的无线调度问题的状态空间进行了缩减，使得在线学习成为可能。所提Online-MGF策略在未知信道统计条件下实现了有效的调度决策，并在ON/OFF信道特殊情形下与经典Whittle指数策略一致，为实际无线网络中的高效资源调度提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider a wireless scheduling problem in downlink wireless networks with unknown channel statistics, where a Base Station (BS) sends data to multiple users. The scheduling performance relies heavily on accurate Channel State Information (CSI), which is often costly to acquire. In this paper, CSI is obtained from ACK/NACK feedback, only after each scheduled transmission. Due to limited wireless channel resources, all users cannot be scheduled for transmission simultaneously. Hence, the most recently observed CSI can be outdated. The traditional approach to solve scheduling problems using outdated CSI is to utilize belief states, which are calculated using the time correlation statistics of channels. However, channel statistics are often unknown; consequently, belief states can be uncountable and this approach becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce a new sufficient statistics for the wireless scheduling problem. Towards this effort, we characterize the CSI staleness by the Age of Channel State Information (AoCSI) and show that the latest observed CSI and its AoCSI is a sufficient statistic of the history to make the scheduling decisions. Accordingly, we are able to reduce the state space for online learning. Our goal is to develop an online scheduling algorithm that maximizes the expected sum throughput of all users over a finite time-horizon while satisfying a channel resource constraint. The formulated problem is a Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB). We develop an online Maximum Gain First (Online-MGF) policy, which achieves sub-linear regret on the number of episodes. For a special case of ON/OFF channels, we are able to prove indexability and derive a closed-form expression of the Whittle index. Numerical results demonstrate that the Online-MGF policy converges to MGF and Whittle index policies with known statistics within a very few episodes.

</details>


### [222] [Empirical Evaluation of Cross-Carrier MCPTT & OTT MCX Interoperability in High-Density Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19554)
*Eman Hammad,Derek Ladd,Sridhar Kowdley,Walt Magnussen,Michael Fox*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过在大规模集会（10.5万观众）的实时环境中测试宽带关键任务一键通（MCPTT）服务，发现语音传输故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈（特别是传输层抖动超标导致缓冲器下溢），而非设备硬件问题；优先管理通道可有效规避此类拥塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享商业基础设施上部署宽带MCPTT服务时，大规模人群事件中的多机构响应会导致资源竞争，需评估跨运营商互操作性和标准与优先QoS框架在真实饱和条件下的表现。

Method: 设计了一项经验性多运营商实地实验：在德克萨斯农工大学凯尔体育场举行10.5万+观众的橄榄球比赛期间，在多个物理区域部署12部相同智能手机，使用POLQA、数据包交付指标和连接率监控自动语音通话。

Result: 语音路径故障仅限于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制；识别出一种急剧、非线性的网络故障模型——当传输层抖动超过临界阈值时，去抖动缓冲器下溢，导致结构性音频降级；优先管理通道能有效规避此类拥塞。

Conclusion: 为应急规划者提供操作性见解：应强制要求网络基础设施、端到端网络切片和专用资源预配，能够将传输层抖动保持在临界故障边界以下。

Abstract: Deploying broadband Mission-Critical Push-To-Talk (MCPTT) services over shared commercial infrastructures introduces resource contention during multi-agency responses in mass-crowd events. This study evaluates cross-carrier interoperability and standard versus prioritized quality of service (QoS) frameworks under real-world saturation constraints. We design an empirical multi-carrier field experiment utilizing twelve identical smartphones deployed across multiple physical sectors inside Texas A&M University's Kyle Field during a football game with 105,000+ attendees. Automated voice calls were monitored using Perceptual Objective Listening Quality Analysis (POLQA), packet delivery metrics, and connection rates. The results reveal that voice path failure is isolated to network infrastructure bottlenecks rather than device hardware limitations. Specifically, we identify a sharp, non-linear network failure model where transport-layer jitter exceeding a critical threshold de-jitter buffer underflows, causing structural audio degradation. Priority-managed channels effectively bypass this congestion. This study helps establish an operational insight for emergency planners to mandate network infrastructure, end-to-end network slicing and dedicated resource provisioning capable of keeping transport-layer jitter below the critical failure boundary.

</details>


### [223] [Vorticity Dissipation Based Routing: A Fluid-Kinetic Framework for Loop-Free Transport in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19630)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于涡度耗散的流体力学路由框架(VDR)，利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观流量正交分解为无旋分量与有旋分量，通过涡度耗散机制消除路由环路，提升超密集无线网络的路由效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超密集无线网络中离散路由协议存在信令开销大和瞬时路由环路问题，影响无线资源效率；现有标量密度连续建模方法缺乏表征这些拓扑异常所需的向量几何结构。

Method: 1. 引入VDR流体力学框架，利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观流量正交解耦为需求驱动的无旋分量和环路诱导的有旋分量
2. 定义网络涡度作为量化拓扑低效的宏观指标
3. 将路由优化表述为能量泛函上的梯度流，推导涡度耗散方程作为控制动力学律
4. 通过Lyapunov稳定性分析证明涡度耗散机制

Result: 数值结果表明：VDR能有效抑制实际转发环路、降低端到端延迟、保持鲁棒的分组交付，在固定区域密集化下呈现近线性扩展性，同时明确考虑了依赖于网格的Poisson求解器成本。

Conclusion: Lyapunov稳定性分析证明该机制确保全局能量单调衰减至渐近无环平衡状态，VDR为超密集无线网络提供了一种可缩放且拓扑高效的路由解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete routing protocols in ultra-dense wireless networks are constrained by signaling overhead and transient routing loops that degrade radio-resource efficiency. While continuum modeling provides a scalable alternative, existing scalar density approaches lack the vector geometric structure required to characterize these topological anomalies. This paper introduces a fluid-kinetic framework, vorticity dissipation-based routing (VDR), utilizing the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition. We demonstrate that the macroscopic traffic flux can be orthogonally decoupled into a demand-driven irrotational component and a loop-induced solenoidal component representing routing vorticity. Building on this insight, we define network vorticity as a macroscopic metric to quantify topological inefficiency. Routing optimization is formulated as a gradient flow on an enstrophy functional, yielding a vorticity dissipation equation as the governing dynamic law. Lyapunov stability analysis proves that this mechanism ensures the monotonic decay of global enstrophy toward an asymptotically loop-free equilibrium. Numerical results validate that VDR suppresses realized forwarding loops, reduces end-to-end delay, maintains robust packet delivery, and exhibits near-linear scaling under fixed-area densification while explicitly accounting for the grid-dependent Poisson-solver cost.

</details>


### [224] [Digital Tides: A Fluid-Dynamic Framework for Flux-Aware Infrastructure Provisioning in UAV Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19638)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于流体的时空框架和通量感知非对称激活策略，通过求解连续性方程获取工作负载流场信息，利用通量向量作为需求传播的运动学前兆，实现UAV蜂群"数字潮汐"场景下的零延迟资源前置，在服务质量约束下达成服务可靠性与能耗的帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频脉冲物流无人机蜂群引发的"数字潮汐"造成复杂交通动态，传统基于静态快照分析和局部密度估计的基础设施资源配置策略无法捕捉计算工作负载的宏观对流现象，导致反应性资源激活存在固有滞后性，在推进波前处造成关键任务服务丢失。

Method: (1)开发流体时空框架：显式求解连续性方程，表征工作负载流的宏观速度场；(2)提出通量感知非对称激活策略：利用导出的信息通量向量作为需求传播的运动学前兆，区别于对称阈值处理，将激活与去激活动态解耦；(3)建立QoS惩罚指标：评估严格中断约束下的有效能效。

Result: 理论分析证实通量信号的固有空间相位超前性，该策略生成主动防护环以补偿服务设置延迟（包括移动边缘计算容器冷启动延迟）；推导出瞬时服务可用性和周期平均能效的闭式表达式；数值结果显示该通量驱动策略实现移动波前零延迟跟踪，在服务可靠性与能耗间达成帕累托最优，优于动态物流走廊中的反应性基线方案。

Conclusion: 基于流体动力学的资源预配置方法通过解耦激活/去激活动态并利用通量信号相位超前特性，有效克服了传统反应式方案的滞后性缺陷，为UAV蜂群密集部署的移动计算网络提供了具有前瞻性的资源保障机制。

Abstract: The emergence of high-frequency pulsating logistics unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms gives rise to ``Digital Tides'', i.e., complex traffic dynamics that challenge sustainable resource provisioning in mobile computing networks. Conventional infrastructure provisioning strategies, which typically rely on static snapshot-based analysis and localized density estimation, fail to capture the macroscopic advection of computational workloads. As a result, reactive resource activation suffers from inherent hysteresis, yielding nominal efficiency gains at the cost of mission-critical service loss at the advancing wavefront. To address this issue, we develop a fluid-based spatiotemporal framework by explicitly solving the continuity equation to characterize the macroscopic velocity field of the workload flow. Building on this framework, we propose a flux-aware asymmetric activation strategy that leverages the derived information flux vector as a kinematic precursor of demand propagation. Unlike symmetric thresholding, the proposed control logic decouples activation and deactivation dynamics. Theoretical analysis confirms the intrinsic spatial phase-lead of the flux signal and shows that the proposed strategy generates a proactive guard ring to compensate for service setup latency, including delays caused by mobile edge computing container cold-starts. We further derive closed-form expressions for instantaneous service availability and period-average energy efficiency. In addition, we formulate a quality-of-service-penalized metric to evaluate effective energy efficiency under strict outage constraints. Numerical results show that the proposed flux-driven strategy enables zero-latency tracking of the mobile wavefront and achieves a Pareto-optimal trade-off between service reliability and energy consumption, outperforming reactive baselines in dynamic logistics corridors.

</details>


### [225] [RFWM: Physics-Guided World Model for Dynamic Wireless Radiance Field Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19709)
*Zijiu Yang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出RFWM物理引导射频世界模型，通过两阶段训练策略实现动态未知环境下的高精度射频场建模，显著提升分布内外性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频辐射场建模对无线网络优化和感知至关重要，但现有学习方法难以泛化到动态和未知环境。

Method: 采用两阶段物理引导训练策略：第一阶段适配预训练视觉扩散骨干预测RF序列（结合Friis引导先验）；第二阶段训练ControlNet并微调骨干网络（使用六个物理引导正则化器），通过跨高度头生成多高度RF轨迹。

Result: 构建包含7,715个序列（跨115环境）的新基准，实验显示在分布内和分布外设置下分别将MSE提升约7 dB和3 dB。

Conclusion: RFWM有效解决了动态未知环境中的射频场建模挑战，通过物理引导实现显著提升的性能表现。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) radiance-field modeling is essential for wireless network optimization and sensing, yet remains challenging in dynamic and unseen environments. Existing learning-based methods synthesize RF fields from sparse measurements, but most struggle to generalize to dynamic and unseen environments. To address this limitation, we propose RFWM, a physics-guided RF world model that maps multimodal physical conditions like visual dynamics and AP configurations to spatiotemporal RF fields. RFWM adopts a two-stage training strategy with physics-guided priors and constraints. In the first stage, RFWM adapts a pretrained visual diffusion backbone to RF trajectories to predict RF sequences from a few past RF inputs, while conditioning the backbone on a Friis-guided prior for coarse attenuation guidance. In the second stage, RFWM learns the physical-to-RF mapping by training a ControlNet from scratch and fine-tuning the RF-adapted backbone, while six physics-guided regularizers enforce fine-grained propagation consistency. Cross-height heads then jointly generate RF trajectories at queried receiver heights in one forward pass. We construct a new benchmark of 7,715 sequences averaging 33 frames across 115 environments for dynamic RF-field generation. Experimental results show that RFWM improves MSE by approximately 7 dB and 3 dB over the state of the art under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [226] [Proper Sea Surface Roughness Enhances the Performance of Near-Shore Maritime Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19730)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Shaoshi Yang,Song Zhao,Jinyang Yu,Weiliang Xie,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个基于随机几何的近岸海事无线通信性能分析框架，通过非齐次泊松点过程建模船舶空间分布，并从瑞利准则推导出与波高相关的新反射系数，实现了粗糙海况下上行链路覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表征，揭示了海况依赖的可靠性-容量权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近岸海事无线通信的准确性能分析对确保可靠运行至关重要，但现有解析模型通常依赖过度简化的传播假设（如完全平滑海面），无法充分捕捉海事信道的完整动态特性。

Method: 1. 采用随机几何理论构建物理意义明确的分析框架；2. 用非齐次泊松点过程对船舶空间分布建模，反映真实近港密度；3. 基于经典瑞利准则推导新颖反射系数，显式关联路径损耗与有效波高；4. 将粗糙度感知信道模型集成到随机几何框架中；5. 推导上行链路覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式。

Result: 1. 首次实现了动态条件下聚合干扰的可行解析表征；2. 揭示了海况依赖的可靠性-容量权衡：粗糙度引起的相干镜面反射衰减可抑制破坏性干扰零点，提升可靠性导向的覆盖性能，但同时降低高SINR和平均速率性能；3. 现有测量数据支持粗糙度敏感的反射机制；4. 跨频率消融验证了粗糙度效应的波长依赖性，证明反射系数需针对工作频率进行评估。

Conclusion: 所提框架为粗糙海况下的海事信道提供了可行的解析表征。海况对可靠性-容量权衡有显著影响，反射系数必须针对工作频率进行评估。VHF频段在粗糙海况下的直接验证尚不可用，相关结果作为基于模型的预测进行解释。

Abstract: Accurate performance analysis for near-shore maritime wireless communication is essential for ensuring robust and reliable operations. However, existing analytical models often rely on oversimplified propagation assumptions, such as a perfectly smooth sea surface, which fail to capture the full dynamics of the maritime channel. In this paper, we develop a physically grounded analytical framework using stochastic geometry that bridges this gap. The spatial distribution of vessels is modeled as a non-homogeneous Poisson point process to reflect realistic near-port densities. We replace the idealized smooth-sea assumption by deriving a novel reflection coefficient from the classical Rayleigh criterion, which explicitly links the path loss to the significant wave height. Integrating this roughness-aware channel model into the stochastic geometry framework, we derive new analytical expressions for the uplink coverage probability and average ergodic rate, providing the first tractable characterization of aggregate interference under such dynamic conditions. The analysis reveals a sea-state-dependent reliability--capacity trade-off: roughness-induced attenuation of the coherent specular reflection can suppress destructive-interference nulls and improve reliability-oriented coverage, while reducing high-SINR and average-rate performance. Available measurements support the underlying roughness-sensitive reflection mechanism, but direct VHF validation under rough sea conditions remains unavailable; the corresponding rough-sea results are therefore interpreted as model-based predictions. A cross-frequency ablation further confirms the wavelength dependence of the roughness effect and shows that the reflection coefficient must be evaluated for the operating frequency.

</details>


### [227] [Using Zone-Disjoint Multi-Path Routing Algorithm for Video Transmission over Ah-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20148)
*Ali Asghar Khavasi,Nastooh Taheri Javan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区域不相交路径的多路径路由算法，用于解决自组网中CSMA/CA效应导致的路径干扰问题，并实现视频多路径传输性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网格拓扑自组网中，尽管多路径路由看似简单，但CSMA/CA介质访问机制导致传统节点不相交路径并非完全独立——中间节点需保持沉默以避免邻域干扰，从而增加端到端延迟，且各路径间的端到端延迟和误码率（BER）相互依赖。研究动机在于设计能够消除此类干扰的多路径路由算法，以改善自组网视频传输性能。

Method: 本文提出一种新的多路径路由算法，采用区域不相交路径（zone-disjoint paths）替代传统节点不相交路径，确保各路径在地理区域上隔离，从而避免邻域通信模式的相互干扰。该算法专门针对视频多路径传输设计，并配合适当的视频传输方案使用。

Result: 实验结果表明，该新算法在视频多路径传输场景下，相较于现有算法获得了显著增强的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过使用区域不相交路径路由算法，可以有效消除自组网中因CSMA/CA机制和邻域干扰导致的路径依赖性问题，为视频多路径传输提供更高性能保障。

Abstract: Finding multi-path routes in ad hoc networks due to their grid topology seems to be a trivial task, but because of CSMA/CA effects in these networks found paths are not completely disjoint unless an appropriate algorithm have taken into account. If such an algorithm provided and designed carefully it could improve multi-path video transmission over these kinds of networks. By using node-disjoint paths, it is expected that the end-to-end delay and BER in each case should be independent of each other. However, because of natural properties and medium access mechanisms in ad hoc networks the end-to-end delay and also BER between any source and destination depends on the pattern of communication in the neighborhood region. In this case some of the intermediate nodes should be silent to reverence their neighbors and this matter increases the end-to-end delay. To avoid this problem, multi-path routing algorithms can use zone-disjoint paths instead of node-disjoint paths. In this paper we demonstrated a new multi-path routing algorithm that selects zone-disjoint paths that in addition used for video multi-path transmission over ad hoc networks. It is shown that by using this new algorithm along with choosing an appropriate scheme for video transmission over discovered paths, our approach receives enhanced results comparing previously used algorithms.
